54	## 0. Git Branch Invariant
55	
56	**Do not create, rename, or switch git branches during plan-phase.** Branch identity is established at discuss-phase and is owned by the user's git workflow. A phase rename in ROADMAP.md is a plan-level change only — it does not mutate git branch names. If `phase_slug` in the init JSON differs from the current branch name, that is expected and correct; leave the branch unchanged.

The planner's `<required_reading>` is extended to include `@/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/.claude/gsd-core/references/tdd.md` so gate enforcement rules are available during planning.

Explicit dependencies always load regardless of recency (e.g., Phase 7 declaring `Depends on: Phase 2` always sees Phase 2's context). Bounded recency keeps the planner's context budget focused on recent work.

87	## 1.5. Closed-Phase Gate (#3569)
88	
89	The init JSON includes `phase_status` — one of `Pending | Planned | In Progress | Executed | Complete | Needs Review`. `Complete` means the phase has all summaries AND a `VERIFICATION.md` with `status: passed`. Replanning a closed phase silently rewrites plan docs that no longer match the shipped code, so the workflow must hard-stop here unless the operator explicitly overrides.
90	
91	Parse `phase_status` from the init JSON, then:
92	
93	```bash
94	FORCE_REPLAN=false
95	if [[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ (^|[[:space:]])--force([[:space:]]|$) ]]; then
96	  FORCE_REPLAN=true
97	fi
98	
99	if [ "${phase_status}" = "Complete" ]; then
100	  if [[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ (^|[[:space:]])--reviews([[:space:]]|$) ]]; then
101	    # --reviews on a closed phase is never legitimate — concerns belong in a
102	    # new phase or issue against the closed phase's commits.
103	    cat <<EOF >&2
104	Phase ${phase_number} (${phase_name}) is already CLOSED (VERIFICATION status: passed).
105	/gsd-plan-phase --reviews cannot replan a closed phase. If the review surfaced
106	real concerns, open a follow-up phase or file an issue against the closed
107	phase's commits. There is no --force override for --reviews on a closed phase.
108	EOF
109	    exit 1
110	  fi
111	  if [ "$FORCE_REPLAN" != "true" ]; then
112	    cat <<EOF >&2
113	Phase ${phase_number} (${phase_name}) is already CLOSED (VERIFICATION status: passed).
114	Replanning a closed phase will overwrite plan docs that no longer match the
115	shipped code. If you intentionally want to replan over closed work, re-run
116	with: /gsd-plan-phase ${phase_number} --force
117	
118	Otherwise, to view what shipped, see: ${verification_path}
119	EOF
120	    exit 1
121	  fi
122	  # FORCE_REPLAN=true: continue, but emit a banner so the operator sees the
123	  # decision in the transcript and in any committed plan docs.
124	  echo "WARNING: Replanning CLOSED phase ${phase_number} under --force. Verify the closeout was wrong before committing new plan docs." >&2
125	fi
126	```
127	
128	The gate fires only on `Complete`. `Executed` and `Needs Review` are not gated — those states mean planning was finished but verification did not pass, and replanning is a legitimate next step.
129	
130	## 2. Parse and Normalize Arguments
131	
132	Extract from $ARGUMENTS: phase number (integer or decimal like `2.1`), flags (`--research`, `--skip-research`, `--research-phase <N>`, `--gaps`, `--skip-verify`, `--skip-ui`, `--prd <filepath>`, `--ingest <path-or-glob>`, `--ingest-format <auto|nygard|madr|narrative>`, `--reviews`, `--text`, `--bounce`, `--skip-bounce`, `--chunked`, `--mvp`, `--tdd`, `--granularity <coarse|standard|fine>`, `--force` (override closed-phase gate, see §1.5)).
133	
134	**`--research-phase <N>` — research-only mode (#3042 + #3044).** When this flag is present, parse `<N>` as the phase number (overrides any positional phase argument), set `RESEARCH_ONLY=true`, and treat the rest of this workflow as a research-dispatch only — the planner spawn (step 8), plan-checker, verification, gaps, bounce, and post-planning-gaps blocks all skip on `RESEARCH_ONLY`. Use this for cross-phase research, doc review before committing to a planning approach, and correction-without-replanning loops. Replaces the deleted `/gsd-research-phase` command.
135	
136	In research-only mode, two modifiers control behavior when `RESEARCH.md` already exists:
137	
138	- **`--research`** — force-refresh re-research without prompting. Re-spawns the researcher unconditionally and overwrites the existing RESEARCH.md. (This is the existing `--research` flag's standard "force re-research" semantics, reused here.)
139	- **`--view`** — view-only: print existing `RESEARCH.md` to stdout, do **not** spawn the researcher. Sets `VIEW_ONLY=true`. Cheapest mode for the correction-without-replanning loop. If `RESEARCH.md` does not exist, error with a hint to drop `--view`.
140	
141	```bash
142	RESEARCH_ONLY=false
143	VIEW_ONLY=false
144	if [[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ --research-phase[[:space:]]+([0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?) ]]; then
145	  RESEARCH_ONLY=true
146	  PHASE="${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
147	fi
148	if $RESEARCH_ONLY && [[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ (^|[[:space:]])--view([[:space:]]|$) ]]; then
149	  VIEW_ONLY=true
150	fi
151	```
152	
153	**`--granularity <coarse|standard|fine>` — CLI override (#703).** When present, this value is the resolved granularity passed to the planner — it wins over any per-phase `granularities.<type>` config, top-level `granularity` config, or project defaults. The init JSON always includes a `granularity` field reflecting the resolved value; read it from there. Invalid values (anything other than `coarse`, `standard`, `fine`) cause an error at the CLI boundary.
154	
155	Set `TEXT_MODE=true` if `--text` is present in $ARGUMENTS OR `text_mode` from init JSON is `true`. When `TEXT_MODE` is active, replace every `AskUserQuestion` call with a plain-text numbered list and ask the user to type their choice number. This is required for Claude Code remote sessions (`/rc` mode) where TUI menus don't work through the Claude App.
156	
157	**MVP_MODE resolution.** Resolve `MVP_MODE` once via the centralized `phase.mvp-mode` query verb. Precedence (first hit wins): CLI flag → ROADMAP.md `**Mode:** mvp` → `workflow.mvp_mode` config → false. The verb is the single source of truth — do not re-implement the chain.
158	
159	```bash
160	MVP_FLAG_ARG=""
161	if [[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ (^|[[:space:]])--mvp([[:space:]]|$) ]]; then MVP_FLAG_ARG="--cli-flag"; fi
162	if [[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ (^|[[:space:]])--tdd([[:space:]]|$) ]]; then TDD_MODE=true; fi
163	```
164	
165	Defer the `phase.mvp-mode` query until `PHASE` is finalized (after explicit argument parsing/fallback phase detection + validation). The verb returns `true|false`; full result also exposes `source` (`cli_flag` | `roadmap` | `config` | `none`) for diagnostics. Mode is **all-or-nothing per phase** (PRD decision Q1).
166	
167	**Walking Skeleton gate.** When `MVP_MODE=true` AND `phase_number == "01"` AND there are zero prior phase summaries (new project), the planner runs in **Walking Skeleton mode** (per PRD decision Q2 — new projects only). Detect with:
168	
169	```bash
170	WALKING_SKELETON=false
171	if [ "$MVP_MODE" = "true" ] && [ "$padded_phase" = "01" ]; then
172	  PRIOR_SUMMARIES=$(gsd_run query phases.list --pick summaries_total 2>/dev/null || echo "0")
173	  if [ "$PRIOR_SUMMARIES" = "0" ]; then WALKING_SKELETON=true; fi
174	fi
175	```
176	
177	When `WALKING_SKELETON=true`:
178	
179	- Planner is instructed to produce `SKELETON.md` in the phase directory alongside `PLAN.md`. The template lives at `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/.claude/gsd-core/references/skeleton-template.md` — the planner reads it when producing SKELETON.md (lazy; not loaded on non-skeleton runs).
180	- The plan must scaffold project + routing + one real DB read/write + one real UI interaction + dev deployment — the thinnest possible end-to-end working slice.
181	
182	**Interaction with `--prd <filepath>`.** `--mvp` and `--prd` compose. The PRD express path (Step 3.5) creates `CONTEXT.md` from the PRD file and continues to research; the Walking Skeleton gate fires independently from the conditions above. When both are active on Phase 1 of a new project, the planner receives `WALKING_SKELETON=true` and PRD-derived context simultaneously — the PRD informs _what the skeleton should prove_. No precedence is needed; the two signals are orthogonal. See [`references/mvp-concepts.md`](../references/mvp-concepts.md) for the broader interaction map.
183	
184	Extract express-path args from $ARGUMENTS: `PRD_FILE` (`--prd <filepath>`), `INGEST_PATH` (`--ingest <path-or-glob>`), and optional `INGEST_FORMAT` (`--ingest-format <auto|nygard|madr|narrative>`, default `auto`).
185	
186	`--prd` and `--ingest` are mutually exclusive. If both are present, error and exit:
187	`Invalid arguments: cannot combine \`--prd\` with \`--ingest\`.`
188	
189	**If no phase number:** Detect next unplanned phase from roadmap.
190	
191	**If `phase_found` is false:** Validate phase exists in ROADMAP.md. If valid, create the directory using `expected_phase_dir` from init (includes `project_code` prefix when set):
192	
193	```bash
194	mkdir -p "${expected_phase_dir}"
195	```
196	
197	Set `phase_dir="${expected_phase_dir}"` after creation.
198	
199	**Existing artifacts from init:** `has_research`, `has_plans`, `plan_count`.
200	
201	Set `CHUNKED_MODE` from flag or config:
202	
203	```bash
204	CHUNKED_CFG=$(gsd_run query config-get workflow.plan_chunked 2>/dev/null || echo "false")
205	CHUNKED_MODE=false
206	if [[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ --chunked ]] || [[ "$CHUNKED_CFG" == "true" ]]; then
207	  CHUNKED_MODE=true
208	fi

232	
233	```bash

245	## 3.5. Handle PRD Express Path
246	
247	**Skip if:** No `--prd` flag in arguments.
248	
249	**If `--prd <filepath>` provided:**
250	
251	1. Read the PRD file:
252	
253	```bash
254	PRD_CONTENT=$(cat "$PRD_FILE" 2>/dev/null)
255	if [ -z "$PRD_CONTENT" ]; then
256	  echo "Error: PRD file not found: $PRD_FILE"
257	  exit 1
258	fi
259	```
260	
261	2. Display banner:
262	
263	```
264	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
265	 GSD ► PRD EXPRESS PATH
266	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
267	
268	Using PRD: {PRD_FILE}
269	Generating CONTEXT.md from requirements...
270	```
271	
272	3. Parse the PRD content and generate CONTEXT.md. The orchestrator should:
273	   - Extract all requirements, user stories, acceptance criteria, and constraints from the PRD
274	   - Map each to a locked decision (everything in the PRD is treated as a locked decision)
275	   - Identify any areas the PRD doesn't cover and mark as "Claude's Discretion"
276	   - **Extract canonical refs** from ROADMAP.md for this phase, plus any specs/ADRs referenced in the PRD — expand to full file paths (MANDATORY)
277	   - Create CONTEXT.md in the phase directory
278	
279	4. Write CONTEXT.md:
280	
281	```markdown
282	# Phase [X]: [Name] - Context
283	
284	**Gathered:** [date]
285	**Status:** Ready for planning
286	**Source:** PRD Express Path ({PRD_FILE})
287

292	
293	</domain>

338	[If none: "None — PRD covers phase scope"]

343	
344	_Phase: XX-name_
345	_Context gathered: [date] via PRD Express Path_
346	```
347	
348	5. Commit:
349	
350	```bash
351	gsd_run query commit "docs(${padded_phase}): generate context from PRD" --files "${phase_dir}/${padded_phase}-CONTEXT.md"
352	```
353	
354	6. Set `context_content` to the generated CONTEXT.md content and continue to step 5 (Handle Research).
355	
356	**Effect:** This completely bypasses step 4 (Load CONTEXT.md) since we just created it. The rest of the workflow (research, planning, verification) proceeds normally with the PRD-derived context.
357	
358	## 3.6. Handle ADR Ingest Express Path
359	
360	**Skip if:** No `--ingest` flag in arguments.
361	
362	**If `--ingest <path-or-glob>` provided:**
363	
364	1. Display banner: `GSD ► ADR Ingest Express Path` with `{INGEST_PATH}` and `{INGEST_FORMAT}`.
365	2. Parse each resolved ADR through `gsd-core/bin/lib/adr-parser.cjs` (`--input`, `--format`) and collect normalized records.
366	3. Status gate: reject `superseded`/`rejected`/`deprecated`; warn on `proposed`; missing status defaults to `accepted`.
367	4. Empty-decisions fallback: if all parsed ADRs have zero `decisions[]`, emit `ADR ingest produced no locked decisions; fall back to discuss-phase for this phase.` and exit with `/gsd-discuss-phase {N}` guidance.
368	5. Generate CONTEXT.md using `<domain>`, `<decisions>`, `<canonical_refs>`, `<specifics>`, `<deferred>`, `<scope_fence>`, map `consequences_positive[]` to Success Criteria and `consequences_negative[]` to Risk Summary, and include `**Source:** ADR Ingest Express Path ({INGEST_PATH})`.
369	6. Commit with `gsd-tools.cjs query commit "docs(${padded_phase}): generate context from ADR ingest" --files "${phase_dir}/${padded_phase}-CONTEXT.md"` and set `context_content`; continue to step 5.
370	
371	**Effect:** This bypasses step 4 (Load CONTEXT.md) since CONTEXT.md was synthesized from ADR input.
372	
373	## 4. Load CONTEXT.md
374	
375	**Skip if:** PRD express path or ADR ingest express path was used (CONTEXT.md already created in step 3.5/3.6).
376	
377	Check `context_path` from init JSON.
378	
379	If `context_path` is not null, display: `Using phase context from: ${context_path}`
380	
381	**If `context_path` is null (no CONTEXT.md exists):**
382	
383	Read discuss mode for context gate label:
384	
385	```bash
386	DISCUSS_MODE=$(gsd_run query config-get workflow.discuss_mode 2>/dev/null || echo "discuss")
387	```
388	
389	If `TEXT_MODE` is true, present as a plain-text numbered list:
390	
391	```
392	No CONTEXT.md found for Phase {X}. Plans will use research and requirements only — your design preferences won't be included.
393	
394	1. Continue without context — Plan using research + requirements only
395	[If DISCUSS_MODE is "assumptions":]
396	2. Gather context (assumptions mode) — Analyze codebase and surface assumptions before planning
397	[If DISCUSS_MODE is "discuss" or unset:]
398	2. Run discuss-phase first — Capture design decisions before planning
399	
400	Enter number:
401	```
402	
403	Otherwise use AskUserQuestion:
404	
405	- header: "No context"
406	- question: "No CONTEXT.md found for Phase {X}. Plans will use research and requirements only — your design preferences won't be included. Continue or capture context first?"
407	- options:
408	  - "Continue without context" — Plan using research + requirements only
409	    If `DISCUSS_MODE` is `"assumptions"`:
410	  - "Gather context (assumptions mode)" — Analyze codebase and surface assumptions before planning
411	    If `DISCUSS_MODE` is `"discuss"` (or unset):
412	  - "Run discuss-phase first" — Capture design decisions before planning
413	
414	If "Continue without context": Proceed to step 5.
415	If "Run discuss-phase first":
416	**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT invoke discuss-phase as a nested Skill/Task call — AskUserQuestion
417	does not work correctly in nested subcontexts (#1009). Instead, display the command
418	and exit so the user runs it as a top-level command:
419	
420	```
421	Run this command first, then re-run /gsd-plan-phase {X} ${GSD_WS}:
422	
423	/gsd-discuss-phase {X} ${GSD_WS}
424	```
425	
426	**Exit the plan-phase workflow. Do not continue.**

429	
430	**Skip if:** `ai_integration_phase_enabled` from config is false, or `--skip-ai-spec` flag provided.

434	AI_PHASE_CFG=$(gsd_run query config-get workflow.ai_integration_phase 2>/dev/null || echo "true")

443	```

452	  - Design an evaluation strategy
453	
454	  Continue planning without AI-SPEC? (non-blocking — /gsd-ai-integration-phase can be run after)

463	If "Continue": Proceed. (Non-blocking — planner will note AI-SPEC is absent.)

465	**If `AI_SPEC_FILE` is non-empty:** Extract framework for planner context:

473	## 5. Handle Research
474	
475	**Skip if:** `--gaps` flag or `--skip-research` flag or `--reviews` flag.
476	
477	### 5.0. Research-Only Modifiers (`--view`, `--research`)
478	
479	**Skip if:** `RESEARCH_ONLY` is `false`.
480	
481	Three branches in research-only mode (`--research-phase <N>`):
482	
483	1. **`--view`**: print `RESEARCH.md` to stdout, no spawn, exit. If `RESEARCH.md` is missing, error with: `--view requires an existing RESEARCH.md; drop --view to spawn the researcher.`
484	2. **`--research`** (force-refresh): re-spawn researcher unconditionally — fall through to "Spawn gsd-phase-researcher" below.
485	3. **Neither flag AND `has_research=true`:** auto-use the existing research and exit cleanly — do not prompt, do not re-spawn. Emit `RESEARCH.md already exists for Phase ${PHASE}, using it. To force-refresh, re-invoke with --research; to print, re-invoke with --view. Path: ${research_path}` then exit. The explicit-flag escape hatches cover any deviation; this matches §5.1's promptless auto-use of existing research, removing the §5.0/§5.1 inconsistency (#159).
486	
487	```bash
488	if [[ "$VIEW_ONLY" == "true" ]]; then
489	  [[ -f "$research_path" ]] || { echo "Error: --view requires an existing RESEARCH.md (Phase ${PHASE}). Drop --view to spawn the researcher."; exit 1; }
490	  cat "$research_path"; exit 0
491	fi
492	```
493	
494	### 5.1. Standard Research Decision
495	
496	**Skip if** `RESEARCH_ONLY=true` (the research-only mode in 5.0 already determined the path: spawn or exit). Without this guard, an LLM following the workflow could fall through into "use existing, skip to step 6" → planner spawn, violating the research-only contract. **CR #3045 finding: this gate makes the early-exit unreachable from any non-research-only branch.**
497	
498	**If `has_research` is true (from init) AND no `--research` flag:** Use existing, skip to step 6.
499	
500	**If RESEARCH.md missing OR `--research` flag:**
501	
502	**If no explicit flag (`--research` or `--skip-research`) and not `--auto`:**
503	Ask the user whether to research, with a contextual recommendation based on the phase:
504	
505	If `TEXT_MODE` is true, present as a plain-text numbered list:
506	
507	```
508	Research before planning Phase {X}: {phase_name}?
509	
510	1. Research first (Recommended) — Investigate domain, patterns, and dependencies before planning. Best for new features, unfamiliar integrations, or architectural changes.
511	2. Skip research — Plan directly from context and requirements. Best for bug fixes, simple refactors, or well-understood tasks.
512	
513	Enter number:
514	```
515	
516	Otherwise use AskUserQuestion:
517	
518	```
519	AskUserQuestion([
520	  {
521	    question: "Research before planning Phase {X}: {phase_name}?",
522	    header: "Research",
523	    multiSelect: false,
524	    options: [
525	      { label: "Research first (Recommended)", description: "Investigate domain, patterns, and dependencies before planning. Best for new features, unfamiliar integrations, or architectural changes." },
526	      { label: "Skip research", description: "Plan directly from context and requirements. Best for bug fixes, simple refactors, or well-understood tasks." }
527	    ]
528	  }
529	])
530	```
531	
532	If user selects "Skip research": skip to step 6.
533	
534	**If `--auto` and `research_enabled` is false:** Skip research silently (preserves automated behavior).
535	
536	Display banner:
537	
538	```
539	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
540	 GSD ► RESEARCHING PHASE {X}
541	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
542	
543	◆ Spawning researcher... (runs in a subagent — no output until it returns, ~1–5 min; expected, not a freeze)
544	```
545	
546	### Spawn gsd-phase-researcher
547	
548	```bash
549	PHASE_DESC=$(gsd_run query roadmap.get-phase "${PHASE}" --pick section)
550	```
551	
552	Research prompt:
553	
554	```markdown
555	<objective>
556	Research how to implement Phase {phase_number}: {phase_name}
557	Answer: "What do I need to know to PLAN this phase well?"
558	</objective>
559	
560	<files_to_read>
561	
562	- {context_path} (USER DECISIONS from /gsd-discuss-phase)
563	- {requirements_path} (Project requirements)
564	- {state_path} (Project decisions and history)
565	  </files_to_read>
566	
567	${AGENT_SKILLS_RESEARCHER}
568	
569	<additional_context>
570	**Phase description:** {phase_description}
571	**Phase requirement IDs (MUST address):** {phase_req_ids}
572	
573	**Project instructions:** Read ./CLAUDE.md if exists — follow project-specific guidelines
574	**Project skills:** Check .claude/skills/ or .agents/skills/ directory (if either exists) — read SKILL.md files, research should account for project skill patterns
575	</additional_context>
576	
577	<output>
578	Write to: {phase_dir}/{phase_num}-RESEARCH.md
579	</output>
580	```
581	
582	```
583	Agent(
584	  prompt=research_prompt,
585	  subagent_type="gsd-phase-researcher",
586	  model="{researcher_model}",
587	  description="Research Phase {phase}"
588	)
589	```
590	
591	> **ORCHESTRATOR RULE — ALL RUNTIMES**: After calling Agent() above, stop working on this task immediately. Do not read more files, edit code, or run tests related to this task while the subagent is active. Wait for the subagent to return its result. This prevents duplicate work, conflicting edits, and wasted context. Only resume when the subagent result is available.
592	
593	### Handle Researcher Return
594	
595	- **`## RESEARCH COMPLETE`:** Display confirmation, continue to step 6
596	- **`## RESEARCH BLOCKED`:** Display blocker, offer: 1) Provide context, 2) Skip research, 3) Abort
597	
598	### Research-Only Early Exit (`--research-phase`)
599	
600	**Skip if:** `RESEARCH_ONLY` is `false` (the default).
601	
602	**If `RESEARCH_ONLY=true`:** the user invoked `/gsd-plan-phase --research-phase <N>` for research-only mode. Do **not** continue to Section 5.5+ (validation strategy, planner, plan-checker, verification, gaps, bounce, post-planning-gaps). Print the research-complete summary and exit cleanly:
603	
604	```text
605	✓ Research-only mode complete (#3042)
606	
607	  Phase:       ${PHASE}
608	  RESEARCH.md: ${research_path}
609	
610	Re-run /gsd-plan-phase ${PHASE} to plan the phase using this research,
611	or /gsd-plan-phase ${PHASE} --research to refresh research and plan.
612	```
613	
614	This exits the workflow. The planner / plan-checker / verifier blocks below are skipped.

616	## 5.5. Create Validation Strategy
617	
618	Skip if `nyquist_validation_enabled` is false OR `research_enabled` is false.
619	
620	If `research_enabled` is false and `nyquist_validation_enabled` is true: warn "Nyquist validation enabled but research disabled — VALIDATION.md cannot be created without RESEARCH.md. Plans will lack validation requirements (Dimension 8)." Continue to step 6.
621	
622	**But Nyquist is not applicable for this run** when all of the following are true:
623	
624	- `research_enabled` is false
625	- `has_research` is false
626	- no `--research` flag was provided
627	
628	In that case: **skip validation-strategy creation entirely**. Do **not** expect `RESEARCH.md` or `VALIDATION.md` for this run, and continue to Step 6.
629	
630	```bash
631	grep -l "## Validation Architecture" "${PHASE_DIR}"/*-RESEARCH.md 2>/dev/null || true
632	```

634	**If found:**
635	
636	1. Read template: `/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/.claude/gsd-core/templates/VALIDATION.md`
637	2. Write to `${PHASE_DIR}/${PADDED_PHASE}-VALIDATION.md` (use Write tool)
638	3. Fill frontmatter: `{N}` → phase number, `{phase-slug}` → slug, `{date}` → current date
639	4. Verify:
640	
641	```bash
642	test -f "${PHASE_DIR}/${PADDED_PHASE}-VALIDATION.md" && echo "VALIDATION_CREATED=true" || echo "VALIDATION_CREATED=false"
643	```
644	
645	5. If `VALIDATION_CREATED=false`: STOP — do not proceed to Step 6
646	6. If `commit_docs`: `commit "docs(phase-${PHASE}): add validation strategy"`
647	
648	**If not found:** Warn and continue — plans may fail Dimension 8.

676	## 5.6. UI Design Contract Gate
677	
678	> Skip if `workflow.ui_phase` is explicitly `false` AND `workflow.ui_safety_gate` is explicitly `false` in `.planning/config.json`. If keys are absent, treat as enabled.

682	UI_GATE_CFG=$(gsd_run query config-get workflow.ui_safety_gate 2>/dev/null || echo "true")
683	```
684	
685	**If both are `false`:** Skip to step 6.
686	
687	Check if phase has frontend indicators:
688	
689	```bash
690	PHASE_SECTION=$(gsd_run query roadmap.get-phase "${PHASE}" 2>/dev/null)
691	# Shell-free word-boundary gate (#3718): Node.js helper — no locale env-var dependency.
692	# Reads via stdin to avoid OS ARG_MAX limits on large phase text.
693	# Resolve the helper against the GSD install dir via RUNTIME_DIR (#448) — NOT the consuming
694	# project's git root — falling back to git toplevel / $HOME/.claude. Exit codes mirror grep (0=UI,1=none).
695	_GSD_RT="${RUNTIME_DIR:-$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel 2>/dev/null || pwd)}"
696	UI_GATE_JS=$(for _c in "$_GSD_RT/gsd-core/bin/lib/ui-safety-gate.cjs" "$_GSD_RT/bin/lib/ui-safety-gate.cjs" "$_GSD_RT/.claude/bin/lib/ui-safety-gate.cjs" "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/.claude/gsd-core/bin/lib/ui-safety-gate.cjs" "/Users/myankelev/Code/random/cipher-box/.claude/bin/lib/ui-safety-gate.cjs"; do [ -f "$_c" ] && { echo "$_c"; break; }; done)
697	if [ -n "$UI_GATE_JS" ]; then printf '%s' "$PHASE_SECTION" | node "$UI_GATE_JS" >/dev/null 2>&1; HAS_UI=$?; else echo "WARN: ui-safety-gate.cjs not found via RUNTIME_DIR/\$HOME (#448) — assuming UI present" >&2; HAS_UI=0; fi
698	```
699	
700	**If `HAS_UI` is 0 (frontend indicators found):**

702	Check for existing UI-SPEC:

706	```
707	
708	**If UI-SPEC.md found:** Set `UI_SPEC_PATH=$UI_SPEC_FILE`. Display: `Using UI design contract: ${UI_SPEC_PATH}`
709	
710	**If UI-SPEC.md missing AND `--skip-ui` flag is present in $ARGUMENTS:** Skip silently to step 6.

712	**If UI-SPEC.md missing AND `UI_GATE_CFG` is `true`:**
713	
714	Read ephemeral chain flag (same field as `check.auto-mode` → `auto_chain_active`):

716	```bash
717	AUTO_CHAIN=$(gsd_run query check auto-mode --pick auto_chain_active 2>/dev/null || echo "false")
718	```
719	
720	**If `AUTO_CHAIN` is `true` (running inside a `--chain` or `--auto` pipeline):**

732	UI_SPEC_PATH="${UI_SPEC_FILE}"
733	```
734	
735	Continue to step 6.

743	▶ Recommended next step:
744	`/gsd-ui-phase {N} ${GSD_WS}` — generate UI design contract before planning
745	───────────────────────────────────────────────
746	Also available:
747	- `/gsd-plan-phase {N} --skip-ui ${GSD_WS}` — plan without UI-SPEC (not recommended for frontend phases)
748	```

754	## 5.7. Schema Push Detection Gate
755	
756	> Detects schema-relevant files in the phase scope and injects a mandatory `[BLOCKING]` schema push task into the plan. Prevents false-positive verification where build/types pass because TypeScript types come from config, not the live database.
757	
758	Check if any files in the phase scope match schema patterns:
759	
760	```bash
761	PHASE_SECTION=$(gsd_run query roadmap.get-phase "${PHASE}" --pick section 2>/dev/null)
762	```
763	
764	Scan `PHASE_SECTION`, `CONTEXT.md` (if loaded), and `RESEARCH.md` (if exists) for file paths matching these ORM patterns:
765	
766	| ORM         | File Patterns                                           |
767	| ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------- |
768	| Payload CMS | `src/collections/**/*.ts`, `src/globals/**/*.ts`        |
769	| Prisma      | `prisma/schema.prisma`, `prisma/schema/*.prisma`        |
770	| Drizzle     | `drizzle/schema.ts`, `src/db/schema.ts`, `drizzle/*.ts` |
771	| Supabase    | `supabase/migrations/*.sql`                             |
772	| TypeORM     | `src/entities/**/*.ts`, `src/migrations/**/*.ts`        |
773	
774	Also check if any existing PLAN.md files for this phase already reference these file patterns in `files_modified`.
775	
776	**If schema-relevant files detected:**
777	
778	Set `SCHEMA_PUSH_REQUIRED=true` and `SCHEMA_ORM={detected_orm}`.
779	
780	Determine the push command for the detected ORM:
781	
782	| ORM         | Push Command                | Non-TTY Workaround                                       |
783	| ----------- | --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- |
784	| Payload CMS | `npx payload migrate`       | `CI=true PAYLOAD_MIGRATING=true npx payload migrate`     |
785	| Prisma      | `npx prisma db push`        | `npx prisma db push --accept-data-loss` (if destructive) |
786	| Drizzle     | `npx drizzle-kit push`      | `npx drizzle-kit push`                                   |
787	| Supabase    | `supabase db push`          | Set `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN` env var                      |
788	| TypeORM     | `npx typeorm migration:run` | `npx typeorm migration:run -d src/data-source.ts`        |
789	
790	Inject the following into the planner prompt (step 8) as an additional constraint:
791

1028	Output consumed by /gsd-execute-phase. Plans need:
1029

The plan-review-convergence source-grounding pass reads this section to exclude newly-created symbols from drift verification; omitting it causes new symbols to be flagged for acknowledgement.
1035	  </downstream_consumer>
1036	
1037	<deep_work_rules>
1038	
1039	## Anti-Shallow Execution Rules (MANDATORY)
1040	
1041	Every task MUST include these fields — they are NOT optional:
1042	
1043	1. **`<read_first>`** — Files the executor MUST read before touching anything. Always include:
1044	   - The file being modified (so executor sees current state, not assumptions)
1045	   - Any "source of truth" file referenced in CONTEXT.md (reference implementations, existing patterns, config files, schemas)
1046	   - Any file whose patterns, signatures, types, or conventions must be replicated or respected
1047	
1048	2. **`<acceptance_criteria>`** — Verifiable conditions that prove the task was done correctly. Rules:
1049	   - Every criterion must be checkable as a source assertion, behavior assertion, test command, or CLI output
1050	   - NEVER use subjective language ("looks correct", "properly configured", "consistent with")
1051	   - Include exact strings, patterns, values, command outputs, or observable behavior where that is the right proof
1052	   - Examples:
1053	     - Code: `auth.py contains def verify_token(` / `test_auth.py exits 0`
1054	     - Behavior: `POST /api/auth/login returns 200 + httpOnly JWT cookie for valid credentials`
1055	     - Config: `.env.example contains DATABASE_URL=` / `Dockerfile contains HEALTHCHECK`
1056	     - Docs: `README.md contains '## Installation'` / `API.md lists all endpoints`
1057	     - Infra: `deploy.yml has rollback step` / `docker-compose.yml has healthcheck for db`
1058	
1059	3. **`<action>`** — Must include CONCRETE values, not references. Rules:
1060	   - NEVER say "align X with Y", "match X to Y", "update to be consistent" without specifying the exact target state
1061	   - Include concrete identifiers and reference values: config keys, function signatures, SQL table names, class names, import paths, env vars, endpoint paths, etc.
1062	   - If CONTEXT.md has a comparison table or expected values, copy only the target identifiers/values needed to remove ambiguity
1063	   - Do not include full file contents, fenced code blocks, or complete implementations in `<action>`
1064	   - The executor should understand the intended target state from `<action>` and use `<read_first>` files for current implementation details, patterns, and source-of-truth context
1065	
1066	**Why this matters:** Executor agents work from the plan text. Vague instructions like "update the config to match production" produce shallow one-line changes. Concrete instructions like "add DATABASE_URL, set POOL_SIZE=20, add REDIS_URL, and read config/runtime.ts before editing" produce complete work without turning the planner into the executor.
1067	</deep_work_rules>

1099	## 8.5. Chunked Planning Mode
1100	
1101	**Skip if `CHUNKED_MODE` is `false`.**
1102	
1103	Chunked mode splits the single long-lived planner Agent run into a short outline Agent run followed by
1104	N short per-plan Agent runs. Each run is bounded to ~3–5 min; each plan is committed individually
1105	for crash resilience. If any run hangs and the terminal is force-killed, rerunning
1106	`/gsd-plan-phase {N} --chunked` resumes from the last successfully committed plan.
1107	
1108	**Intended for new or in-progress chunked runs.** To recover plans already written by a prior
1109	_non-chunked_ run, use step 6's "Add more plans" or proceed directly to `/gsd-execute-phase`
1110	— don't start a fresh chunked run over existing non-chunked plans.
1111	
1112	### 8.5.1 Outline Phase (outline-only mode, ~2 min)
1113	
1114	**Resume detection:** If `${PHASE_DIR}/${PADDED_PHASE}-PLAN-OUTLINE.md` already exists **and
1115	is valid** (contains the `## OUTLINE COMPLETE` marker), skip this sub-step — the outline
1116	already exists from a previous run. Proceed directly to 8.5.2.
1117	
1118	```bash
1119	OUTLINE_FILE="${PHASE_DIR}/${PADDED_PHASE}-PLAN-OUTLINE.md"
1120	if [[ -f "$OUTLINE_FILE" ]] && grep -q "^## OUTLINE COMPLETE" "$OUTLINE_FILE"; then
1121	  # reuse existing outline — skip to 8.5.2
1122	fi
1123	```
1124	
1125	Display:
1126	
1127	```text
1128	◆ Chunked mode: spawning outline planner... (runs in a subagent — no output until it returns, ~1–5 min; expected, not a freeze)
1129	```

1144	
1145	  Return: ## OUTLINE COMPLETE with plan count.",

This prevents duplicate work, conflicting edits, and wasted context. Only resume when the subagent result is available.

1164	   valid YAML frontmatter** (opening `---` delimiter present), skip this plan (do not
1165	   overwrite completed work — resume safety).

1169	   if [[ -f "$PLAN_FILE" ]] && head -1 "$PLAN_FILE" | grep -q '^---'; then

1211	## 9. Handle Planner Return
1212	
1213	- **`## PLANNING COMPLETE`:** Display plan count. If `--skip-verify` or `plan_checker_enabled` is false (from init): skip to step 13. Otherwise: step 10.
1214	- **`## PHASE SPLIT RECOMMENDED`:** The planner determined the phase exceeds the context budget for full-fidelity implementation of all source items. Handle in step 9b.
1215	- **`## ⚠ Source Audit: Unplanned Items Found`:** The planner's multi-source coverage audit found items from REQUIREMENTS.md, RESEARCH.md, ROADMAP goal, or CONTEXT.md decisions that are not covered by any plan. Handle in step 9c.
1216	- **`## CHECKPOINT REACHED`:** Present to user, get response, spawn continuation (step 12)
1217	- **`## PLANNING INCONCLUSIVE`:** Show attempts, offer: Add context / Retry / Manual
1218	- **Empty / truncated / no recognized marker:** → Filesystem fallback (step 9a).
1219	
1220	## 9a. Filesystem Fallback (Planner)
1221	
1222	**Triggered when:** Agent() returns but the return contains no recognized marker (`## PLANNING COMPLETE`, `## PHASE SPLIT RECOMMENDED`, `## ⚠ Source Audit`, `## CHECKPOINT REACHED`, `## PLANNING INCONCLUSIVE`).
1223	
1224	```bash
1225	DISK_PLANS=$(ls "${PHASE_DIR}"/*-PLAN.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
1226	```
1227	
1228	**If `DISK_PLANS` > 0:** The planner wrote plans to disk but the Agent() return was empty or
1229	truncated (the Windows stdio hang pattern — the subagent finished but the return never
1230	arrived). Display:
1231	
1232	```text
1233	◆ Planner wrote {DISK_PLANS} plan(s) to disk but did not emit a PLANNING COMPLETE marker.
1234	  This is a known Windows stdio hang pattern — work is likely recoverable.
1235	
1236	  Plans found on disk:
1237	  {ls output of *-PLAN.md}
1238	```
1239	
1240	Offer 3 options:
1241	
1242	1. **Accept plans** — treat as `## PLANNING COMPLETE` and continue through step 9 `## PLANNING COMPLETE` handling (so `--skip-verify` / `plan_checker_enabled=false` are honored — may skip to step 13 rather than step 10)
1243	2. **Retry planner** — re-spawn the planner with the same prompt (return to step 8)
1244	3. **Stop** — exit; user can re-run `/gsd-plan-phase {N}` to resume
1245	
1246	**If `DISK_PLANS` is 0 and no marker:** The planner produced no output. Treat as
1247	`## PLANNING INCONCLUSIVE` and handle accordingly.
1248	
1249	## 9b. Handle Phase Split Recommendation
1250	
1251	When the planner returns `## PHASE SPLIT RECOMMENDED`, it means the phase's source items exceed the context budget for full-fidelity implementation. The planner proposes groupings.
1252	
1253	**Extract from planner return:**
1254	
1255	- Proposed sub-phases (e.g., "17a: processing core (D-01 to D-19)", "17b: billing + config UX (D-20 to D-27)")
1256	- Which source items (REQ-IDs, D-XX decisions, RESEARCH items) go in each sub-phase
1257	- Why the split is necessary (context cost estimate, file count)
1258	
1259	**Present to user:**
1260	
1261	```
1262	## Phase {X} exceeds context budget for full-fidelity implementation
1263	
1264	The planner found {N} source items that exceed the context budget when
1265	planned at full fidelity. Instead of reducing scope, we recommend splitting:
1266	
1267	**Option 1: Split into sub-phases**
1268	- Phase {X}a: {name} — {items} ({N} source items, ~{P}% context)
1269	- Phase {X}b: {name} — {items} ({M} source items, ~{Q}% context)
1270	
1271	**Option 2: Proceed anyway** (planner will attempt all, quality may degrade past 50% context)
1272	
1273	**Option 3: Prioritize** — you choose which items to implement now,
1274	rest become a follow-up phase
1275	```
1276	
1277	Use AskUserQuestion with these 3 options.
1278	
1279	**If "Split":** Use `/gsd-phase --insert` to create the sub-phases, then replan each.
1280	**If "Proceed":** Return to planner with instruction to attempt all items at full fidelity, accepting more plans/tasks.
1281	**If "Prioritize":** Use AskUserQuestion (multiSelect) to let user pick which items are "now" vs "later". Create CONTEXT.md for each sub-phase with the selected items.
1282	
1283	## 9c. Handle Source Audit Gaps
1284	
1285	When the planner returns `## ⚠ Source Audit: Unplanned Items Found`, it means items from REQUIREMENTS.md, RESEARCH.md, ROADMAP goal, or CONTEXT.md decisions have no corresponding plan.
1286	
1287	**Extract from planner return:**
1288	
1289	- Each unplanned item with its source artifact and section
1290	- The planner's suggested options (A: add plan, B: split phase, C: defer with confirmation)
1291	
1292	**Present each gap to user.** For each unplanned item:
1293	
1294	```
1295	## ⚠ Unplanned: {item description}
1296	
1297	Source: {RESEARCH.md / REQUIREMENTS.md / ROADMAP goal / CONTEXT.md}
1298	Details: {why the planner flagged this}
1299	
1300	Options:
1301	1. Add a plan to cover this item (recommended)
1302	2. Split phase — move to a sub-phase with related items
1303	3. Defer — add to backlog (developer confirms this is intentional)
1304	```
1305	
1306	Use AskUserQuestion for each gap (or batch if multiple gaps).
1307	
1308	**If "Add plan":** Return to planner (step 8) with instruction to add plans covering the missing items, preserving existing plans.
1309	**If "Split":** Use `/gsd-phase --insert` for overflow items, then replan.
1310	**If "Defer":** Record in CONTEXT.md `## Deferred Ideas` with developer's confirmation. Proceed to step 10.
1311	
1312	## 10. Spawn gsd-plan-checker Agent
1313	
1314	Display banner:
1315	
1316	```
1317	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
1318	 GSD ► VERIFYING PLANS
1319	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
1320	
1321	◆ Spawning plan checker... (runs in a subagent — no output until it returns, ~1–5 min; expected, not a freeze)
1322	```
1323	
1324	Checker prompt:
1325	
1326	```markdown
1327	<verification_context>
1328	**Phase:** {phase_number}
1329	**Phase Goal:** {goal from ROADMAP}
1330	
1331	<files_to_read>
1332	
1333	- {PHASE_DIR}/\*-PLAN.md (Plans to verify)
1334	- {roadmap_path} (Roadmap)
1335	- {requirements_path} (Requirements)
1336	- {context_path} (USER DECISIONS from /gsd-discuss-phase)
1337	- {research_path} (Technical Research — includes Validation Architecture)
1338	  </files_to_read>
1339	
1340	${AGENT_SKILLS_CHECKER}
1341	
1342	**Phase requirement IDs (MUST ALL be covered):** {phase_req_ids}
1343	
1344	**Project instructions:** Read ./CLAUDE.md if exists — verify plans honor project guidelines
1345	**Project skills:** Check .claude/skills/ or .agents/skills/ directory (if either exists) — verify plans account for project skill rules
1346	</verification_context>
1347	
1348	<expected_output>
1349	
1350	- ## VERIFICATION PASSED — all checks pass
1351	- ## ISSUES FOUND — structured issue list
1352	  </expected_output>
1353	```
1354	
1355	```
1356	Agent(
1357	  prompt=checker_prompt,
1358	  subagent_type="gsd-plan-checker",
1359	  model="{checker_model}",
1360	  description="Verify Phase {phase} plans"
1361	)
1362	```
1363	
1364	> **ORCHESTRATOR RULE — ALL RUNTIMES**: After calling Agent() above, stop working on this task immediately. Do not read more files, edit code, or run tests related to this task while the subagent is active. Wait for the subagent to return its result. This prevents duplicate work, conflicting edits, and wasted context. Only resume when the subagent result is available.
1365	
1366	## 11. Handle Checker Return
1367	
1368	- **`## VERIFICATION PASSED`:** Display confirmation, proceed to step 13.
1369	- **`## ISSUES FOUND`:** Display issues, check iteration count, proceed to step 12.
1370	- **Empty / truncated / no recognized marker:** → Filesystem fallback (step 11a).
1371	
1372	**Thinking partner for architectural tradeoffs (conditional):**
1373	If `features.thinking_partner` is enabled, scan the checker's issues for architectural tradeoff keywords
1374	("architecture", "approach", "strategy", "pattern", "vs", "alternative"). If found:
1375	
1376	```
1377	The plan-checker flagged an architectural decision point:
1378	{issue description}
1379	
1380	Brief analysis:
1381	- Option A: {approach_from_plan} — {pros/cons}
1382	- Option B: {alternative_approach} — {pros/cons}
1383	- Recommendation: {choice} aligned with {phase_goal}
1384	
1385	Apply this to the revision? [Yes] / [No, I'll decide]
1386	```
1387	
1388	If yes: include the recommendation in the revision prompt. If no: proceed to revision loop as normal.
1389	If thinking_partner disabled: skip this block entirely.
1390	
1391	## 11a. Filesystem Fallback (Checker)
1392	
1393	**Triggered when:** Checker Agent() returns but the return contains neither `## VERIFICATION PASSED` nor `## ISSUES FOUND`.
1394	
1395	```bash
1396	DISK_PLANS=$(ls "${PHASE_DIR}"/*-PLAN.md 2>/dev/null | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
1397	```
1398	
1399	**If `DISK_PLANS` > 0:** Plans exist on disk; the checker return was empty or truncated (the
1400	Windows stdio hang pattern — the subagent finished but the return never arrived). Display:
1401	
1402	```text
1403	◆ Checker return was empty or truncated. {DISK_PLANS} plan(s) exist on disk.
1404	  This is a known Windows stdio hang pattern — checker may have completed without returning.
1405	```
1406	
1407	Offer 3 options:
1408	
1409	1. **Accept verification** — treat as `## VERIFICATION PASSED` and continue to step 13
1410	2. **Retry checker** — re-spawn the checker with the same prompt (return to step 10)
1411	3. **Stop** — exit; user can re-run `/gsd-plan-phase {N}` to resume
1412	
1413	**If `DISK_PLANS` is 0:** No plans on disk — something is seriously wrong. Display error and stop.
1414	
1415	## 12. Revision Loop (Max 3 Iterations)
1416	
1417	Track `iteration_count` (starts at 1 after initial plan + check).
1418	Track `prev_issue_count` (initialized to `Infinity` before the loop begins).
1419	Track `stall_reentry_count` (starts at 0; incremented each time "Adjust approach" re-enters step 8).
1420	
1421	**If iteration_count < 3:**
1422	
1423	Parse issue count from checker return: count BLOCKER + WARNING entries in the YAML issues block (structured output from gsd-plan-checker). If the checker's return contains no YAML issues block (i.e., the plan was approved with no issues), treat `issue_count` as 0 and skip the stall check — the plan passed. Proceed to step 13.
1424	
1425	Display: `Revision iteration {N}/3 -- {blocker_count} blockers, {warning_count} warnings`
1426	
1427	**Stall detection:** If `issue_count >= prev_issue_count`:
1428	Display: `Revision loop stalled — issue count not decreasing ({issue_count} issues remain after {N} iterations)`
1429	
1430	**If `stall_reentry_count < 2`:**
1431	Ask user:
1432	Question: "Issues remain after {N} revision attempts with no progress. Proceed with current output?"
1433	Options: "Proceed anyway" | "Adjust approach"
1434	If "Proceed anyway": accept current plans and continue to step 13.
1435	If "Adjust approach": increment `stall_reentry_count`, open freeform discussion, then re-enter step 8 (full replanning). Note: re-entry resets `iteration_count` and `prev_issue_count` but `stall_reentry_count` persists across re-entries and is capped at 2.
1436	
1437	**If `stall_reentry_count >= 2`:**
1438	Display: `Stall persists after 2 re-planning attempts. The following issues could not be resolved automatically:`
1439	List the remaining issues from the checker.
1440	Suggest: "Consider resolving these issues manually or running `/gsd-debug` to investigate root causes."
1441	Options: "Proceed anyway" | "Abandon"
1442	If "Proceed anyway": accept current plans and continue to step 13.
1443	If "Abandon": stop workflow.
1444	
1445	Set `prev_issue_count = issue_count`.
1446	
1447	Revision prompt:
1448	
1449	```markdown
1450	<revision_context>
1451	**Phase:** {phase_number}
1452	**Mode:** revision
1453	
1454	<files_to_read>
1455	
1456	- {PHASE_DIR}/\*-PLAN.md (Existing plans)
1457	- {context_path} (USER DECISIONS from /gsd-discuss-phase)
1458	  </files_to_read>
1459	
1460	${AGENT_SKILLS_PLANNER}
1461	
1462	**Checker issues:** {structured_issues_from_checker}
1463	</revision_context>
1464	
1465	<instructions>
1466	Make targeted updates to address checker issues.
1467	Do NOT replan from scratch unless issues are fundamental.
1468	Return what changed.
1469	</instructions>
1470	```
1471	
1472	```
1473	Agent(
1474	  prompt=revision_prompt,
1475	  subagent_type="gsd-planner",
1476	  model="{planner_model}",
1477	  description="Revise Phase {phase} plans"
1478	)
1479	```
1480	
1481	> **ORCHESTRATOR RULE — ALL RUNTIMES**: After calling Agent() above, stop working on this task immediately. Do not read more files, edit code, or run tests related to this task while the subagent is active. Wait for the subagent to return its result. This prevents duplicate work, conflicting edits, and wasted context. Only resume when the subagent result is available.
1482	
1483	After planner returns -> spawn checker again (step 10), increment iteration_count.
1484	
1485	**If iteration_count >= 3:**
1486	
1487	Display: `Max iterations reached. {N} issues remain:` + issue list
1488	
1489	Offer: 1) Force proceed, 2) Provide guidance and retry, 3) Abandon
1490	
1491	## 12.5. Plan Bounce (Optional External Refinement)
1492	
1493	**Skip if:** `--skip-bounce` flag, `--gaps` flag, or bounce is not activated.
1494	
1495	**Activation:** Bounce runs when `--bounce` flag is present OR `workflow.plan_bounce` config is `true`. The `--skip-bounce` flag always wins (disables bounce even if config enables it). The `--gaps` flag also disables bounce (gap-closure mode should not modify plans externally).
1496	
1497	**Prerequisites:** `workflow.plan_bounce_script` must be set to a valid script path. If bounce is activated but no script is configured, display warning and skip:
1498	
1499	```
1500	⚠ Plan bounce activated but no script configured.
1501	Set workflow.plan_bounce_script to the path of your refinement script.
1502	Skipping bounce step.
1503	```
1504	
1505	**Read pass count:**
1506	
1507	```bash
1508	BOUNCE_PASSES=$(gsd_run query config-get workflow.plan_bounce_passes 2>/dev/null || echo "2")
1509	BOUNCE_SCRIPT=$(gsd_run query config-get workflow.plan_bounce_script 2>/dev/null | jq -r '.' 2>/dev/null || true)
1510	```
1511	
1512	Display banner:
1513	
1514	```
1515	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
1516	 GSD ► BOUNCING PLANS (External Refinement)
1517	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
1518	
1519	Script: ${BOUNCE_SCRIPT}
1520	Max passes: ${BOUNCE_PASSES}
1521	```
1522	
1523	**For each PLAN.md file in the phase directory:**
1524	
1525	1. **Backup:** Copy `*-PLAN.md` to `*-PLAN.pre-bounce.md`
1526	
1527	```bash
1528	cp "${PLAN_FILE}" "${PLAN_FILE%.md}.pre-bounce.md"
1529	```
1530	
1531	2. **Invoke bounce script:**
1532	
1533	```bash
1534	"${BOUNCE_SCRIPT}" "${PLAN_FILE}" "${BOUNCE_PASSES}"
1535	```
1536	
1537	3. **Validate bounced plan — YAML frontmatter integrity:**
1538	   After the script returns, check that the bounced file still has valid YAML frontmatter (opening and closing `---` delimiters with parseable content between them). If the bounced plan breaks YAML frontmatter validation, restore the original from the pre-bounce.md backup and continue to the next plan:
1539	
1540	```
1541	⚠ Bounced plan ${PLAN_FILE} has broken YAML frontmatter — restoring original from pre-bounce backup.
1542	```
1543	
1544	4. **Handle script failure:** If the bounce script exits non-zero, restore the original plan from the pre-bounce.md backup and continue to the next plan:
1545	
1546	```
1547	⚠ Bounce script failed for ${PLAN_FILE} (exit code ${EXIT_CODE}) — restoring original from pre-bounce backup.
1548	```
1549	
1550	**After all plans are bounced:**
1551	
1552	5. **Re-run plan checker on bounced plans:** Spawn gsd-plan-checker (same as step 10) on all modified plans. If a bounced plan fails the checker, restore original from its pre-bounce.md backup:
1553	
1554	```
1555	⚠ Bounced plan ${PLAN_FILE} failed checker validation — restoring original from pre-bounce backup.
1556	```
1557	
1558	6. **Commit surviving bounced plans:** If at least one plan survived both the frontmatter validation and the checker re-run, commit the changes:
1559	
1560	```bash
1561	gsd_run query commit "refactor(${padded_phase}): bounce plans through external refinement" --files "${PHASE_DIR}/*-PLAN.md"
1562	```
1563	
1564	Display summary:
1565	
1566	```
1567	Plan bounce complete: {survived}/{total} plans refined
1568	```
1569	
1570	**Clean up:** Remove all `*-PLAN.pre-bounce.md` backup files after the bounce step completes (whether plans survived or were restored).
1571	
1572	## 13. Requirements Coverage Gate
1573	
1574	After plans pass the checker (or checker is skipped), verify that all phase requirements are covered by at least one plan.
1575	
1576	**Skip if:** `phase_req_ids` is null or TBD (no requirements mapped to this phase).
1577	
1578	**Step 1: Extract requirement IDs claimed by plans**
1579	
1580	```bash
1581	# Collect all requirement IDs from plan frontmatter
1582	PLAN_REQS=$(grep -h "requirements_addressed\|requirements:" ${PHASE_DIR}/*-PLAN.md 2>/dev/null | tr -d '[]' | tr ',' '\n' | sed 's/^[[:space:]]*//' | sort -u)
1583	```
1584	
1585	**Step 2: Compare against phase requirements from ROADMAP**
1586	
1587	For each REQ-ID in `phase_req_ids`:
1588	
1589	- If REQ-ID appears in `PLAN_REQS` → covered ✓
1590	- If REQ-ID does NOT appear in any plan → uncovered ✗
1591	
1592	**Step 3: Check CONTEXT.md features against plan objectives**
1593	
1594	Read CONTEXT.md `<decisions>` section. Extract feature/capability names. Check each against plan `<objective>` blocks. Features not mentioned in any plan objective → potentially dropped.
1595	
1596	**Step 4: Report**
1597	
1598	If all requirements covered and no dropped features:
1599	
1600	```
1601	✓ Requirements coverage: {N}/{N} REQ-IDs covered by plans
1602	```
1603	
1604	→ Proceed to step 14.
1605	
1606	If gaps found:
1607	
1608	```
1609	## ⚠ Requirements Coverage Gap
1610	
1611	{M} of {N} phase requirements are not assigned to any plan:
1612	
1613	| REQ-ID | Description | Plans |
1614	|--------|-------------|-------|
1615	| {id} | {from REQUIREMENTS.md} | None |
1616	
1617	{K} CONTEXT.md features not found in plan objectives:
1618	- {feature_name} — described in CONTEXT.md but no plan covers it
1619	
1620	Options:
1621	1. Re-plan to include missing requirements (recommended)
1622	2. Move uncovered requirements to next phase
1623	3. Proceed anyway — accept coverage gaps
1624	```
1625	
1626	If `TEXT_MODE` is true, present as a plain-text numbered list (options already shown in the block above). Otherwise use AskUserQuestion to present the options.
1627	
1628	## 13a. Decision Coverage Gate
1629	
1630	After the requirements coverage gate passes, verify that every trackable
1631	decision captured by discuss-phase in CONTEXT.md `<decisions>` is referenced
1632	by at least one plan. This is the **translation gate** from issue #2492 —
1633	its job is to refuse to mark a phase planned when a discuss-phase decision
1634	silently dropped on the way into the plans.
1635	
1636	**Skip if** `workflow.context_coverage_gate` is explicitly set to `false`
1637	(absent key = enabled). Also skip if no CONTEXT.md exists for this phase
1638	(nothing to translate) or if its `<decisions>` block is empty.
1639	
1640	```bash
1641	GATE_CFG=$(gsd_run query config-get workflow.context_coverage_gate 2>/dev/null || echo "true")
1642	if [ "$GATE_CFG" != "false" ]; then
1643	  GATE_RESULT=$(gsd_run query check.decision-coverage-plan "${PHASE_DIR}" "${CONTEXT_PATH}")
1644	  # BLOCKING: refuse to mark phase planned when a trackable decision is uncovered.
1645	  # `passed: true` covers both real-pass and skipped cases (gate disabled / no CONTEXT.md /
1646	  # no trackable decisions). Verify-phase counterpart deliberately omits this exit-1 — that
1647	  # gate is non-blocking by design (review finding F15).
1648	  echo "$GATE_RESULT" | jq -e '(.passed // .data.passed) == true' >/dev/null || {
1649	    echo "$GATE_RESULT" | jq -r '(.message // .data.message // "Decision coverage gate failed.")'
1650	    exit 1
1651	  }
1652	fi
1653	```
1654	
1655	The handler returns JSON:
1656	
1657	```json
1658	{
1659	  "passed": true,
1660	  "skipped": false,
1661	  "total": 2,
1662	  "covered": 2,
1663	  "uncovered": [{ "id": "D-01", "text": "...", "category": "..." }],
1664	  "message": "..."
1665	}
1666	```
1667	
1668	**If `passed` is true (or `skipped` is true):** Display
1669	`✓ Decision coverage: {M}/{N} CONTEXT.md decisions covered by plans` (or
1670	`(skipped — gate disabled)` / `(skipped — no decisions)`) and proceed to
1671	step 13b.
1672	
1673	**If `passed` is false:** Display the handler's `message` block. It already
1674	names each uncovered decision (`D-NN | category | text`) and tells the user
1675	what to do — cite the id in a relevant plan's `must_haves` / `truths`, or
1676	move the decision under `### Claude's Discretion` / tag it `[informational]`
1677	if it should not be tracked. Then offer:
1678	
1679	```text
1680	Options:
1681	1. Re-plan to cover missing decisions (recommended)
1682	2. Edit CONTEXT.md to mark dropped decisions as [informational] / Discretion
1683	3. Proceed anyway — accept the coverage gap
1684	```
1685	
1686	If `TEXT_MODE` is true, present as a plain-text numbered list. Otherwise use
1687	AskUserQuestion. Selecting "Proceed anyway" continues to step 13b but
1688	records the override in STATE.md so verify-phase can re-surface it.
1689	
1690	**Why this gate blocks:** failing here is cheap. The plans are the contract
1691	between discuss-phase and execute-phase; if a decision isn't visible in any
1692	plan, no executor will implement it. Catching that now beats discovering it
1693	after thousands of dollars of execution.
1694	
1695	## 13b. Record Planning Completion in STATE.md
1696	
1697	After plans pass all gates, record that planning is complete so STATE.md reflects the new phase status:
1698	
1699	```bash
1700	gsd_run query state.planned-phase --phase "${PHASE_NUMBER}" --name "${PHASE_NAME}" --plans "${PLAN_COUNT}"
1701	```
1702	
1703	This updates STATUS to "Ready to execute", sets the correct plan count, and timestamps Last Activity.
1704	
1705	## 13c. Annotate ROADMAP with Wave Dependencies and Cross-cutting Constraints
1706	
1707	After plans are finalized, annotate the ROADMAP.md plan list for this phase with:
1708	
1709	- **Wave dependency notes** — a bold header before each wave group ("Wave 2 _(blocked on Wave 1 completion)_")
1710	- **Cross-cutting constraints** — a "Cross-cutting constraints:" subsection listing `must_haves.truths` entries that appear in 2 or more plans
1711	
1712	This step is derived entirely from existing PLAN frontmatter — no extra LLM pass is required.
1713	
1714	```bash
1715	gsd_run query roadmap.annotate-dependencies "${PHASE_NUMBER}"
1716	```
1717	
1718	This operation is idempotent: if wave headers or cross-cutting constraints already exist in the ROADMAP phase section, the command returns without modifying the file. Skip this step if `plan_count` is 0.
1719	
1720	## 13d. Commit Plans if commit_docs is true
1721	
1722	If `commit_docs` is true (from the init JSON parsed in step 1), commit the generated plan artifacts (including any ROADMAP.md annotations from step 13c):
1723	
1724	```bash
1725	gsd_run query commit "docs(${PADDED_PHASE}): create phase plan" --files "${PHASE_DIR}"/*-PLAN.md .planning/STATE.md .planning/ROADMAP.md
1726	```
1727	
1728	This commits all PLAN.md files for the phase plus the updated STATE.md and ROADMAP.md to version-control the planning artifacts. Skip this step if `commit_docs` is false.
1729	
1730	## 13e. Post-Planning Gap Analysis
1731	
1732	After all plans are generated, committed, and the Requirements Coverage Gate (§13)
1733	has run, emit a single unified gap report covering both REQUIREMENTS.md and the
1734	CONTEXT.md `<decisions>` section. This is a **proactive, post-hoc report** — it
1735	does not block phase advancement and does not re-plan. It exists so that any
1736	requirement or decision that slipped through the per-plan checks is surfaced in
1737	one place before execution begins.
1738	
1739	**Skip if:** `workflow.post_planning_gaps` is `false`. Default is `true`.
1740	
1741	```bash
1742	POST_PLANNING_GAPS=$(gsd_run query config-get workflow.post_planning_gaps --default true 2>/dev/null || echo true)
1743	if [ "$POST_PLANNING_GAPS" = "true" ]; then
1744	  # Scope to this phase's mapped REQ-IDs (#447); null/TBD skips the requirements comparison (CONTEXT.md decisions still reported), mirroring §13.
1745	  gsd_run gap-analysis --phase-dir "${PHASE_DIR}" --phase-req-ids "$(gsd_run query init.plan-phase "$PHASE" --pick phase_req_ids 2>/dev/null || echo TBD)"
1746	fi
1747	```
1748	
1749	(`gsd-tools.cjs gap-analysis` reads `.planning/REQUIREMENTS.md`, `${PHASE_DIR}/CONTEXT.md`,
1750	and `${PHASE_DIR}/*-PLAN.md`, then prints a markdown table with one row per
1751	REQ-ID and D-ID. Word-boundary matching prevents `REQ-1` from being mistaken for
1752	`REQ-10`.)
1753	
1754	**Output format (deterministic; sorted REQUIREMENTS.md → CONTEXT.md, then natural
1755	sort within source):**
1756	
1757	```
1758	## Post-Planning Gap Analysis
1759	
1760	| Source | Item | Status |
1761	|--------|------|--------|
1762	| REQUIREMENTS.md | REQ-01 | ✓ Covered |
1763	| REQUIREMENTS.md | REQ-02 | ✗ Not covered |
1764	| CONTEXT.md | D-01 | ✓ Covered |
1765	| CONTEXT.md | D-02 | ✗ Not covered |
1766	
1767	⚠ N items not covered by any plan
1768	```
1769	
1770	**Skip-gracefully behavior:**
1771	
1772	- REQUIREMENTS.md missing → CONTEXT-only report.
1773	- CONTEXT.md missing → REQUIREMENTS-only report.
1774	- Both missing or `<decisions>` block missing → "No requirements or decisions to check" line, no error.
1775	
1776	This step is non-blocking. If items are reported as not covered, the user may
1777	re-run `/gsd-plan-phase --gaps` to add plans, or proceed to execute-phase as-is.
1778	
1779	## 14. Present Final Status
1780	
1781	Route to `<offer_next>` OR `auto_advance` depending on flags/config.
1782	
1783	## 15. Auto-Advance Check
1784	
1785	Check for auto-advance trigger using values already loaded in step 1:
1786	
1787	1. Parse `--auto` and `--chain` flags from $ARGUMENTS
1788	2. Use `auto_chain_active` and `auto_advance` from the INIT JSON parsed in step 1 — **do not issue additional `config-get` calls for these values** (they are already present in the init output). Issuing redundant `config-get` calls for values already in INIT can cause infinite read loops on some runtimes.
1789	3. **Sync chain flag with intent** — if user invoked manually (no `--auto` and no `--chain`), clear the ephemeral chain flag from any previous interrupted `--auto` chain. This does NOT touch `workflow.auto_advance` (the user's persistent settings preference):
1790	   ```bash
1791	   if [[ ! "$ARGUMENTS" =~ --auto ]] && [[ ! "$ARGUMENTS" =~ --chain ]]; then
1792	     gsd_run query config-set workflow._auto_chain_active false || true
1793	   fi
1794	   ```
1795	
1796	Set local variables from INIT (parsed once in step 1):
1797	
1798	- `AUTO_CHAIN` = `auto_chain_active` from INIT JSON (boolean, default false)
1799	- `AUTO_CFG` = `auto_advance` from INIT JSON (boolean, default false)
1800	
1801	**If `--auto` or `--chain` flag present AND `AUTO_CHAIN` is not true:** Persist chain flag to config (handles direct invocation without prior discuss-phase):
1802	
1803	```bash
1804	if ([[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ --auto ]] || [[ "$ARGUMENTS" =~ --chain ]]) && [[ "$AUTO_CHAIN" != "true" ]]; then
1805	  gsd_run query config-set workflow._auto_chain_active true
1806	fi
1807	```
1808	
1809	**If `--auto` or `--chain` flag present OR `AUTO_CHAIN` is true OR `AUTO_CFG` is true:**
1810	
1811	Display banner:
1812	
1813	```
1814	━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━━
1815	 GSD ► AUTO-ADVANCING TO EXECUTE

1817	
1818	Plans ready. Launching execute-phase...
1819	```
1820	
1821	Launch execute-phase using the Skill tool to avoid nested Task sessions (which cause runtime freezes due to deep agent nesting):

1823	```
1824	Skill(skill="gsd-execute-phase", args="${PHASE} --auto --no-transition ${GSD_WS}")
1825	```
1826	
1827	The `--no-transition` flag tells execute-phase to return status after verification instead of chaining further. This keeps the auto-advance chain flat — each phase runs at the same nesting level rather than spawning deeper Task agents.

1904	   Get-Process node -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object {$_.StartTime -lt (Get-Date).AddHours(-1)} | Stop-Process -Force
1905	   ```
1906	3. **Clean up stale task directories:**
1907	   ```powershell
1908	   # Remove stale subagent task dirs (Claude Code never cleans these on crash)
1909	   Remove-Item -Recurse -Force "$env:USERPROFILE\.claude\tasks\*" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
1910	   ```
1911	4. **Reduce MCP server count:** Temporarily disable non-essential MCP servers in settings.json
1912	5. **Retry:** Restart Claude Code and run `/gsd-plan-phase` again
1913	
1914	If freezes persist, try `--skip-research` to reduce the agent chain from 3 to 2 agents:

```
1	# Revision Loop Pattern
2	
3	Standard pattern for iterative agent revision with feedback. Used when a checker/validator finds issues and the producing agent needs to revise its output.
4	
5	---
6	
7	## Pattern: Check-Revise-Escalate (max 3 iterations)
8	
9	This pattern applies whenever:
10	
11	1. An agent produces output (plans, imports, gap-closure plans)
12	2. A checker/validator evaluates that output
13	3. Issues are found that need revision
14	
15	### Flow
16	
17	```
18	prev_issue_count = Infinity
19	iteration = 0
20	
21	LOOP:
22	  1. Run checker/validator on current output
23	  2. Read checker results
24	  3. If PASSED or only INFO-level issues:
25	     -> Accept output, exit loop
26	  4. If BLOCKER or WARNING issues found:
27	     a. iteration += 1
28	     b. If iteration > 3:
29	        -> Escalate to user (see "After 3 Iterations" below)
30	     c. Parse issue count from checker output
31	     d. If issue_count >= prev_issue_count:
32	        -> Escalate to user: "Revision loop stalled (issue count not decreasing)"
33	     e. prev_issue_count = issue_count
34	     f. Re-spawn the producing agent with checker feedback appended
35	     g. After revision completes, go to LOOP
36	```
37	
38	### Issue Count Tracking
39	
40	Track the number of BLOCKER + WARNING issues returned by the checker on each iteration. If the count does not decrease between consecutive iterations, the producing agent is stuck and further iterations will not help. Break early and escalate to the user.
41	
42	Display iteration progress before each revision spawn:
43	`Revision iteration {N}/3 -- {blocker_count} blockers, {warning_count} warnings`
44	
45	### Re-spawn Prompt Structure
46	
47	When re-spawning the producing agent for revision, pass the checker's YAML-formatted issues. The checker's output contains a `## Issues` heading followed by a YAML block. Parse this block and pass it verbatim to the revision agent.
48	
49	```
50	<checker_issues>
51	The issues below are in YAML format. Each has: dimension, severity, finding,
52	affected_field, suggested_fix. Address ALL BLOCKER issues. Address WARNING
53	issues where feasible.
54	
55	{YAML issues block from checker output -- passed verbatim}
56	</checker_issues>
57	
58	<revision_instructions>
59	Address ALL BLOCKER and WARNING issues identified above.
60	- For each BLOCKER: make the required change
61	- For each WARNING: address or explain why it's acceptable
62	- Do NOT introduce new issues while fixing existing ones
63	- Preserve all content not flagged by the checker
64	This is revision iteration {N} of max 3. Previous iteration had {prev_count}
65	issues. You must reduce the count or the loop will terminate.
66	</revision_instructions>
67	```
68	
69	### After 3 Iterations
70	
71	If issues persist after 3 revision cycles:
72	
73	1. Present remaining issues to the user
74	2. Use gate prompt (pattern: yes-no from `references/gate-prompts.md`):
75	   question: "Issues remain after 3 revision attempts. Proceed with current output?"
76	   header: "Proceed?"
77	   options:
78	   - label: "Proceed anyway" description: "Accept output with remaining issues"
79	   - label: "Adjust approach" description: "Discuss a different approach"
80	3. If "Proceed anyway": accept current output and continue
81	4. If "Adjust approach" or "Other": discuss with user, then re-enter the producing step with updated context
82	
83	### Workflow-Specific Variations
84	
85	| Workflow      | Producer Agent | Checker Agent    | Notes                                   |
86	| ------------- | -------------- | ---------------- | --------------------------------------- |
87	| plan-phase    | gsd-planner    | gsd-plan-checker | Revision prompt via planner-revision.md |
88	| execute-phase | gsd-executor   | gsd-verifier     | Post-execution verification             |
89	| discuss-phase | orchestrator   | gsd-plan-checker | Inline revision by orchestrator         |
90	
91	---
92	
93	## Important Notes
94	
95	- **INFO-level issues are always acceptable** -- they don't trigger revision
96	- **Each iteration gets a fresh agent spawn** -- don't try to continue in the same context
97	- **Checker feedback must be inlined** -- the revision agent needs to see exactly what failed
98	- **Don't silently swallow issues** -- always present the final state to the user after exiting the loop
99	
```

===== PHASE SECTION =====
### Phase 60: IPNS Verification Cross-Layer Closeout: Desktop and API

**Goal:** Extend verified IPNS resolution beyond the FUSE crate to the remaining desktop Tauri resolve sites, and recover the per-operation IPNS signature-verification CPU cost on the API publish/resolve hot path — without weakening the zero-knowledge integrity model (untrusted / DHT-sourced records must still be fully verified).
**Requirements**: HARD-11
**Depends on:** Phase 58 (resolve_ipns_verified chokepoint), Phase 59 (unified first-publish sequence convention)
**Plans:** TBD (run /gsd-plan-phase 60)

Scope (captured todos):

- [ ] Route `apps/desktop/src-tauri` `resolve_ipns` sites (`prepopulate.rs` ~43/110/177/236, `vault.rs` ~21/250) through a verified resolver with scoped fail-closed parity — `2026-06-22-desktop-resolve-ipns-verified-coverage.md`
- [ ] Cache / short-circuit redundant IPNS signature verification on the API publish/resolve hot path (`apps/api/src/ipns/ipns.service.ts`): skip re-verifying DB-authoritative records this server just signed/persisted, and/or a short-TTL verified-record cache keyed by `(ipnsName, sequenceNumber, signature)`; MUST still verify externally-sourced / DHT records (someguy resolves) — `2026-06-23-cache-redundant-ipns-signature-verification-hot-path.md`

- [ ] TBD (run /gsd-plan-phase 60 to break down)

1	---
2	created: 2026-06-23T15:56:53.000Z
3	title: Investigate caching/short-circuiting redundant IPNS signature verification on the publish/resolve hot path
4	area: perf
5	severity: low
6	source: GitHub issue #549 (migrated to file-todo) — 2026-06-23 staging upload-throughput re-baseline
7	files:
8	  - apps/api/src/ipns/ipns.service.ts
9	---
10	
11	## Context
12	
13	A 2026-06-23 staging load-test re-baseline (`upload-throughput @ 50 clients`) found upload throughput
14	~10 ops/s vs the 15.10 ops/s Phase 19.2 baseline, on the same 2-vCPU staging box.
15	
16	Root cause was investigated (see `docs/CAPACITY.md` §1.5 and PR #548): the regression is **per-operation
17	CPU cost, not op count or accumulated state**. Ruled out:
18	
19	- **Object/pin count** — a GC (294,811 → 20,038 objects) and an account cleanup (pin set 17,880 → 308)
20	  both failed to move throughput.
21	- **Op count** — constant (3 pins + 2 IPNS publishes per upload; the per-file IPNS model predates the
22	  baseline).
23	- **Kubo version / someguy** — 0.40 → 0.42 gave no gain; someguy has run `SOMEGUY_DHT=accelerated`
24	  since before the baseline.
25	
26	The cause is **IPNS signature verification + durability hardening added after the baseline**: #448
27	(IPNS signature storage + verification, 2026-04-04), #529 (signedRecord validation/verification
28	hardening), #543 (write-path durability hardening), #544 (verify-coverage chokepoint + CAS gate).
29	Every publish/resolve now performs Ed25519 verification + validation + durability fsync + CAS gating,
30	so each operation costs more CPU on a 2-core box.
31	
32	## Opportunity
33	
34	The API frequently **signs** an IPNS record on publish and then **verifies** records on the resolve
35	path — likely re-verifying records it just produced and persisted. Investigate caching or
36	short-circuiting redundant verification of records the API itself just signed/wrote (the DB is
37	authoritative), **without weakening the zero-knowledge integrity model**.
38	
39	## Scope / questions
40	
41	- Map where verification happens: the signed-record verify chokepoint from #544, `resolveRecord` in
42	  `apps/api/src/ipns/ipns.service.ts`, and the publish path.
43	- Profile the publish/resolve hot path to confirm the magnitude of the per-op verification cost.
44	- Propose a safe short-circuit, e.g. skip re-verifying DB-authoritative records this server just
45	  signed/persisted, while **still** verifying externally-sourced / DHT records (someguy resolves) and
46	  anything not produced by this server. Must not reduce integrity guarantees for untrusted inputs.
47	- Consider a short-TTL verified-record cache keyed by `(ipnsName, sequenceNumber, signature)`.
48	
49	## Acceptance
50	
51	A proposal (ideally with a measured per-op cost estimate and a prototype) that recovers verification
52	CPU on the publish/resolve hot path without weakening IPNS integrity verification of untrusted /
53	DHT-sourced records.
54	
55	## References
56	
57	- `docs/CAPACITY.md` §1.5 — re-baseline findings
58	- PR #548 — Kubo tuning + the writeup
59	- Hot-path PRs: #448, #529, #543, #544
60

Publish only *verifies* (`ipns.service.ts:87-89`); resolve does *not even verify* — it returns the bytes for the client to verify.\n- **The client**, which derives the plaintext key (`packages/core/src/vault/init.ts:79,114`) and re-signs on its next publish.\n- **The TEE worker**, which decrypts the wrapped key in-enclave, signs, and zeroes it (`apps/tee-worker/src/routes/republish.ts:71-94`).\n\n---\n\n## 2. The population that needs migrating\n\n**Who embeds 0 (live):** only the two FUSE interactive folder-create paths:\n- `crates/fuse/src/write_ops/implementation/mkdir.rs:173-174` (`create_ipns_record(&ipns_key_arr, &value, 0, 86_400_000)`)\n- `crates/fuse/src/platform/windows/write_ops.rs:201-202` (identical)\n\nThe TS SDK does **not** embed 0 for folders — its folder first-publish computes `newSeq = 0n + 1n = 1n` (`packages/sdk-core/src/cas.ts`, `registration.ts:244`; confirmed by `bin/index.ts:224`). So the **first** TEE cycle on an embedded-0 FUSE folder signs at `1 + 1 = 2`, writes an embedded-2 record, and persists `2` to both tables.
